# Project Change Log

Records the goal, key changes, validation, and handoff information for each task. Append entries in chronological order at the end of the file. See `AGENTS.md` for maintenance rules. This file is for development handoffs and is separate from program runtime logs in directories such as `logs/`.

## Entry template

```markdown
## YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss Europe/London — Brief task title

- Log ID: YYYYMMDD-HHmmss-task
- Git status: Uncommitted (after a successful commit, append the log ID and full commit hash association separately)
- Executor: agent or tool name
- Goal and reason: Why the change was made and what it is intended to solve.
- Key changes: Files involved, behavior changes, and the basis for important decisions.
- Validation: Commands actually run and their results; if none were run, state why.
- Unverified and follow-up: Limitations, blockers, or next steps; if none, write “None”.
```

## 2026-11-05 14:13:49 Europe/London — Gate diagnostic grasp stages and record IK rejection causes

- Log ID: 20261105-141349-feedback-gated-grasp-ik
- Git status: Uncommitted.
- Executor: Codex
- Goal and reason: The user requested that failed approach attempts stop before closure, and that intended-object grasp feedback be confirmed before object-lift tests. Previous trials continued to close and lift after rejected approaches, producing empty-gripper movement instead of held-object lift.
- Key changes: Added optional post-skill stopping to `SkillCollector`, after raw skill logging and before any episode reset; default training collection behavior is unchanged. The diagnostic `ScriptedProvider` ends an attempt after a failed/incomplete primitive or an unconfirmed grasp, using the existing four-consecutive-closed-grasp-control-tick criterion for the intended object. Added per-attempt outcomes, actual control counts, and `feedback_gated_v1` provenance. Finished early attempts are not reopened on resume; old-protocol datasets are rejected to prevent mixing changed sampling behavior. Failed zero-duration skills remain in raw logs. Updated README usage notes. The generic lift primitive, planner search parameters, goals, seeds, units, thresholds, and event-label definitions were not changed.
- IK diagnostics: Each of the existing 16 initial seeds now records solver failure, nonfinite output, joint-limit violation, FK mismatch, collision contact pairs, or acceptance. Failed IK details and successful plan metadata carry these records and counts; no additional IK/collision calls or random draws were introduced. The existing TOPP failure classification remains unchanged.
- Validation: Initial collector/actor/calibration regression passed 54 tests. After adding actual-budget and protocol-resume cases, the final collector/actor command (`python -m pytest -q tests/diagnostics/test_collect_wm_validation.py tests/raps/test_raps_gnn_actor.py`) passed 43 tests. The native-free planner module (`python -m pytest -q tests/environments/test_motion_planning.py`) passed 22 tests. Tests cover failure persistence before stopping, suppression of closure/lift after failure, wrong/insufficient/lost grasps, verified grasp continuation, actual budget accounting, and protocol isolation. The state uses all 77 default objects, stable identities, numeric dynamic flags, sin/cos angles and parent-receptacle multi-hot relations (19028 dimensions). CEM selects eight navigation/posture or six interaction action types and resolves each member's future target from its own predicted state. No task-target preference, auto-navigation, forced success, retry, prediction clipping or task reward was added. Put retains `placeStationary=True` and excludes the held object as receptacle. Local no-target failures are counted attempts without Unity calls; ordinary simulator failures remain data.

Existing cabinet defaults still attach original iCEM. Handled model-only rollout buffer restoration and explicit planner-checkpoint incompatibility. No vendored upstream implementation, ManiSkill, RAPS or PointWorld source was modified by this task.

Source/versions/evaluation hashes, contracts, replay, optimizer/normalizers/RNG/CEM RNG and round counters are saved. Resume is at episode boundaries; offline evaluation does not start Unity.

Sent SIGINT to verified hidden128 training PID453715/session57669; log ends KeyboardInterrupt. Last completed baseline evaluation/checkpoint is1800 attempts/round8. - Model: New exploration/cee_us/thor_mixed_model.py subclasses the vendored forward model without editing upstream sources. Retains5 independent members, edge messages, mean aggregation,2 shared hidden layers per object/global readout; replaces each readout's final linear layer with separate regression/classification linear heads. Added an optional forward_model_class injection to CEEUSBackend, defaulting to original class. The original mse variant remains the default research baseline.
- Scientific protocol: Agent dimensions0:7 and object position/rotation plus openness use normalized continuous-delta MSE. Agent standing/success,12 object flags and multi-hot parents use absolute next-state BCE-with-logits; liquid4 and temperature3 use separate cross-entropies, preserving all encoded categories. Regression=(mean agent continuous MSE+mean object continuous MSE)/2. Classification=equal mean of standing/success/flags/parents/liquid/temperature mean losses. Total=(regression+classification).sum over members. These fixed initial group weights, not validation-tuned values, intentionally change the objective. Only continuous deltas are denormalized/added; sigmoid/softmax probabilities directly form next-state classes; static attributes copy exactly. Existing CEM thresholds and sum-of-ensemble-standard-deviations remain, now on decoded probabilities, so curiosity magnitudes/loss values are not comparable to MSE baseline. No changes to learning rate/Adam settings, action semantics, attention, dataset splits or precision.
- Integration: cee_us_thor.py adds --prediction-mode mse|mixed, checkpoint mode metadata, mode inference on restore, explicit mismatch errors and legacy-missing-key=MSE behavior. Source fingerprints include new model file; manifest records objective and decoding. Old checkpoint inference is supported, but exact resume after source changes still requires original source and is not bypassed. README documents protocol and bounded command.
- Tests: Runtime .runtime/thor-ceeus/bin/python -s -m pytest tests/cee_us/test_thor_mixed_model.py tests/cee_us/test_thor_world_model.py -q passed13; test_thor_training.py passed10. Coverage includes raw next-state vs delta class targets, losses/gradient direction, normalized continuous decoding, binary/category probabilities, exact static copying, optimizer ownership of new heads, checkpoint prediction parity, manual multistep CEM score parity including remainder chunks, mode inference/mismatch and unchanged baseline. ## 2026-11-09 18:20:41 Europe/London — Optional GNS-inspired dynamic sparse THOR graph

- Git status: Uncommitted

- User requested implementing a GNS flag and explicitly selected a0.5m estimated box-gap threshold. Added exploration/cee_us/thor_gns.py without modifying upstream sources or adding dependencies. This is a THOR-specific adaptation inspired by GNS radius rebuilding, not a reproduction of its particle simulator.
- Scientific definition: Each forward call independently rebuilds each ensemble member/sample graph from current inputs, including model-predicted rollout states. Undirected spatial candidates use Euclidean distance between approximate boxes (object.position as center, canonical initial AABB sizes), union bidirectional current parent relations and held-object/encoded Put-target relations; remove self-loops. Raw states/probabilities use0.5 relation/held/action thresholds. No next-state labels or true future geometry enter construction. All nodes/global agent/action context remain. Local/global means divide by retained-edge counts, isolated aggregates are zero. Canonical boxes are approximate, not current THOR colliders; swept contact/opening motion are not guaranteed covered. Graph changes intentionally change the prediction function; no learned-accuracy benefit claimed.
- Implementation reuses the existing edge/object/global MLP parameters, mixed heads and optimizer. Packs selected edges independently per member, padding to maximum member edge-list length; discarded pairs never enter message MLP. Pairwise geometry remains quadratic and padding/construction overhead remains. Added --gns/--no-gns and --gns-radius to online and offline entry points. Full graph stays default for new runs; both mse and mixed supported. Manifest, online/offline checkpoints and source snapshots record graph settings/source. Online restore infers saved graph, rejects explicit mode/radius mismatch; legacy missing graph metadata means full. Existing exact-resume source hash checks remain; no baseline checkpoint graph conversion or offline resume added.

Explicit place Z arguments fail instead of being silently ignored. `domain/robocasa/primitives.py` maps place to shared slots [0,1]; the total action remains 5D, and existing canonicalization zeros the inactive third slot. Strict action-contract checks reject version 4 and earlier checkpoints despite the unchanged vector dimension.
- Geometry: `CabinetMotionPlanner.placement_bounds` in `domain/robocasa/motion_planning.py` reuses exact plane-support calculations for all existing supported payload collision shapes and their local poses. At confirmed grasp, preserve the measured TCP-to-object pose and collision bounds evaluated at the fixed placement orientation. Compute touch TCP by subtracting the collision-center XY and bottom Z offsets from requested object XY/lower-shelf Z, then approach 4 cm above predicted contact. A place started with an already confirmed payload captures a reference before moving. Reset/new empty-hand grasp clears it; carrying and place retain it. Requested object XY, predicted touch TCP, reference and geometry failure diagnostics are recorded. Existing contact/stability/opening, binding, slip, orientation, collision and descent checks remain active.
- Bounds: Object center XY retains X[.370,.671], Y[-.070,.310] root meters (8 cm center margin). Also reject any predicted full payload footprint that would extend within 2 cm of nominal cabinet XY edges; preserve closure on rejection. Bounds are geometric constraints and do not certify IK/path feasibility. No object-type lookup, target-object selection, teleportation or changes to physics, reward, seeds, budgets or baseline interfaces.
- Documentation: README.md and docs/pick_place_primitives.md describe the new semantics, measured geometry dependency, margins and checkpoint incompatibility. Previous cup/bowl successful videos remain explicitly version 4 historical evidence, not validation of the changed version 5 entry route.
- Validation: Existing `apptainer exec --nv /data/data/siyao/3d_world_model.sif env PYTHONPATH=/tmp/raps-three-arm-ls76ex80/numpy126:$PWD OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS=1 OMP_NUM_THREADS=1 python -m pytest -q` runtime. - Git status: Uncommitted. Executor: Codex, with bounded scout and quick_implementer assistance. User approved the discussed new/history replay and stronger protection of historical dynamics. - exploration/raps/protected_replay.py samples original eligible macro indices with replacement using checkpointed global NumPy RNG. New means matching current collection stage; other eligible records are history. Per nominal125-row group,63new/62historical draws; history targets50/25/25 dynamic/contact/static with deterministic largest-remainder rounding (all strata available:31/16/15). Empty strata redistribute proportionally; absent new/history donates quota. Bootstrap uses all new. Dynamic means ANY object's endpoint XYZ displacement>=.015m; otherwise contact means recorded finger force>0 or grasped during the macro, else static. Failed macros can be dynamic. No original records removed. Each pool chooses occupied action-branch/physicalZ5mm/initial7armjoint5degree bins uniformly, then a record uniformly in that bin. Groups computed once per fit.
